1b1('dul ba): @#//'dul ba rnam par 'byed pa//bam po bzhi bcu rtsa gsum pa//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rgyal po'i kha ba na 'od ma'i tshal 
1b2('dul ba): ka lan dka'i gnas na bzhugs so//gang gi tshe tshe dang ldan pa gyang bu nar dge 'dun gyis gnas ma la 'bog pa dang /zas la sko bar bskos pa'i tshe na 
1b3('dul ba): dge slong mdza' bo dang /sa las skyes gnyis tshe dang ldan pa gyang bu nor dang tshe rabs tshe rabs nas kho na yod pas dge slong mdza' bo dang //sa las skyes 
1b4('dul ba): gnyis tshe dang ldan pa nye dga' dang lhan cig du 'du 'dzi'i gtam gyis 'khod pa na tshe dang ldan pa gyang bu nod phyogs der 'ongs pa dang de gnyis kyis nye dga' la smras pa/
1b5('dul ba): tshe dang ldan pa nye dga' gal te khyod kyang dge 'dun gyis gnas mal 'bogs pa dang /zas la sko ba bskos par gyur na/khyod kyang kho bo cag la 'di lta ste dper na tshe 
1b6('dul ba): dang ldan pa gyang bu nor bzhin du tho 'tsham par 'gyur ro//nye dgas de gnyis la smras pa/tshe dang ldan pa gnyis khyed gnyis dge 'dun 'thun pas dge slong gang gnas mal 
1b7('dul ba): 'bogs pa dang /zas las sko bar bskos par shes bzhin du de la dor 'phya'am/de gnyis kyis smras pa/ci kho bo cag gnyis 'dur yang mi dbang ngam/dge slong don nyung

2a1('dul ba): @#//gtong bar byed/kha zer bar byed pa'i skabs de dge slong rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la gsol ba dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/dge slong dag khyed kyis dge slong mdza' bo dang /sa las skyes gnyis sam/
2a2('dul ba): gzhan yang de lta bu gang yin pa de ngor 'phya ba'i grangs su chug shig/'di ltar grangs su gzhug par bya ste/gnas mal bshams pa byas nas/gaN DI brdungs te dris pa'i tshig gis dge slong rnams la yang dag par bsgo nas dge 'dun 
2a3('dul ba): thams cad 'dug cing 'thun par gyur ba la dge slong gcig gis gsel par bya ste/dge 'dun btsun pa rnams gsan du gsol/dge slong mdza' bo dang //sa las skyes 'di gnyis 'tshal bzhin du dge slong gang dge 'dun 'thun pas gnas 
2a4('dul ba): mal 'bog pa dang /zas la sko bar bskos pa tshe dang ldan pa gyang bu nor de la dor 'phyan/gal te dge 'dun gyis dus la bab cing bzod na dge 'dun gyis gnang bar mdzod cig dang /dge 'dun gyis dge slong mdza' bo dang/sa las skyes dong 'phya 
2a5('dul ba): ba'i grangs su stsal to//'di ni gsol ba'o//gang gi tshe dge 'dun gyis dge slong mdzo' bo dang //sa las skye gnyis ngor 'phya ba'i grangs su bcug pa de'i tshe na de gnyis tshe dang ldan pa nye dga' dang lhan cig gtam zer zhing 'dug 
2a6('dul ba): pa na/tshe dang ldan pa gyang bu no ra phyogs der 'ongs ba dang /de gnyis kyis nye dga' la smras pa/tshe dang ldan pa nye dga' gal te khyod kyang dge 'dun gyis gnas mal 'bog pa dang /zas lo sko bar bskos par gyur na/khyod 
2a7('dul ba): kyang kho bo cag la 'di lta ste dper na kha cig bzhin du tho 'tsham par 'gyur ro//nye dgas de gnyis la smras pa/tshe dang ldan ba gnyis khyed gnyis dge 'dun 'thun pas dge slong gang gnas mal 'bogs pa dang /zas la sko bar bskos 
2a8('dul ba): par shes bzhin du de la gzhog 'phyas byed dam/de gnyis kyis smras pa/ci kho bo cag gnyis kyis 'ga' zhig gi ming dam rus nas smos sam/nye dgas smras pa/gal te ming dang rus ma smos par gzhog 'phyas byed du gnang na/
2b1('dul ba): nye dgas kyang 'jig rten thams cad la ming dang dus mi smos pa nga gzhog 'phyas byas par 'gyur ro//dge slong don nyung du dag ci'i phyir dge slong dag dge 'dun 'thun pas/dge slong gang gnas mal 'bog pa dang /zas la sko bar bskos par shes 
2b2('dul ba): bzhin du de la gzhog 'phyas byed ces smod par byed/phyar ka gtong bar byed/kha zer bar byed pa'i skabs de dge slong rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la gsol ba dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/dge slong dag khyed kyis dge slong 
2b3('dul ba): mdza' bo dang /sa las skyes gnyis sam gzhan yang de lta bu gang yin pa de gzhog 'phyas byed pa'i grangs su chug shig/'di ltar grangs su gzhug par bya ste/gnas mal bshams pa byas nas gaN DA brdungs te dris pa'i tshig gis dge 
2b4('dul ba): slong rnams la yang dag par bsgo nas dge 'dun thams cad 'dug cig 'thun par gyur pa la dge slong gcig gis gsol ba bya ste/dge 'dun btsun pa rnams gsan du gsol/dge slong mdza' bo dang /sa las skye sa 'di gnyis 'tshal bzhin du 
2b5('dul ba): dge slong gang dge 'dun 'thun pas gnas mal 'bog pa dang /zas la sko bar bskos pa tshe dang ldan pa gyang bu nor de la gzhog 'phyas bgyis na/gal te dge 'dun gyi dus la bab cing bzod na dge 'dun gyis gnang bar mdzod cig dang /dge 'dun gyis 
2b6('dul ba): dge slong mdza' bo dang //sa las skyes gzhog 'phyas bgyid pa'i grangs su stsal to//'di ni gsol ba'o//gang gi tshe dge 'dun gyis dge slong mdza' bo dang sa las skyes dor 'phya ba dang /gzhog 'phyas byed pa'i grangs su bcug pa de'i 
2b7('dul ba): tshe na de gnyis tshe dang ldan pa gyang bu rnor la ngor 'phya ba dang /gzhog pa phyas byed nas/dge slong don nyung du dag ci'i phyir dge slong dag dge 'dun 'thun pas dge slong gang gnas mal 'bog pa dang/zas la sko bar bskos bar shes bzhin du 
2b8('dul ba): de la ngor 'phya ba dang //gzhog 'phyas byed ces smod par byed/phyar ka gtong bar byed/kha zer bar byed pa'i skabs de dge slong rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la gsol to//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis byung ba 'di dang /gleng gzhi 'di

3b6('dul ba): 'phyas byed na nye byas su 'gyur ro//'phyi ba dang gzhog 'phyas byed na nye byas su gyur ro//dge slong dge bdun gyis ma bgrangs shing /de yang gang zag bcu gnyid su ma bskos pa sbyor ba ma btang ba dag 'di la 'phya na nyes byas su gyur ro//gzhog

4a1('dul ba): @#//pa rdzogs s+ho//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das kau sham+bi na gdangs can gyi kun dga' ra ba na bzhugs so//de'i tshe tshe dang ldan pa 'dun pa ltung ba chos bzhin du phyir mi byed nas/de la dge slong don 'dod pa/bde ba 'dod pa/bde 
4a2('dul ba): ba la reg par 'dod pa/grub pa dang bde ba 'dod pa rnams kyis de la smras pa/tshe dang ldan pa 'dun pa chos bzhin du phyir gyis shig/des smras pa/su la ltung ba byung ba de phyir byed par 'gyur ro//khyod la ltung ba byung ba su zhig phyir byed par 
4a3('dul ba): 'gyur/su 'gyod par 'gyur ba'o//khyod la ltung ba byungab la su 'gyod par 'gyur/gal te tshe dang ldan pa khyed cag la ltung ba byung na kho bo'i mdun du ma 'chags par gzhan gyi mdun du shog shig/tshe dang ldan pa dag khyed cag ni rigs sna tshogs dang 
4a4('dul ba): rus sna tshogs dang /khyim sna tshogs nas rab tu byung ba dag yin te/'di lta ste dper na shing ljon pa dag gi lo ma sna tshogs rlung gis brlags pa dag gcig tu ltung zhing gcig tu 'dug pa de bzhin du khyed cag kyang rigs sna tshogs dang /rus 
4a5('dul ba): sna tshogs dang /khyim sna chogs nas rab tu byung ba de dag yin gyi/kho bo'i 'phags pas ni byang chub thugs su chud pa yin no zhes de dge slong rnams kyis bsgo ba rna la gzon par byed nas/dge slong don nyung ngu dag ci'i phyir dge slong dag dge 
4a6('dul ba): slong rnams kyi bsgo ba rna la gzon pa byed ces smod par byed/phyar ka gtong bar byed/kha zer bar byed pa'i skabs de dge slong rnams kyis gcom ldan 'das la gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/dge slong dag khyed 
4a7('dul ba): kyis dge slong 'dun pa dang gzhan yang de lta bu gang yin pa la bsgo ba rna la gzon pa'i grangs su chug shig/'di ltar grangs su gzhug par bya ste/gnas mal bshams pa byas nas gaN DI brdungs te dris pa'i tshig gis dge slong rnams 
4a8('dul ba): la yang dag par sgo nas dge 'dun thams cad 'dug cing 'thun par gyur pa la dge slong gcig gis gsol ba bya ste/dge 'dun btsun pa rnams gsan du gsol/tshe dang ldan pa 'dun pa 'di ltung ba byung yang chos bzhin du slar mi bgyid 
4b1('dul ba): de/'di la dge slong don 'tshal ba/phan pa 'tshal ba/bde ba 'tshal ba/bde ba la reg par 'tshal ba/grub pa dang bde ba 'tshal ba rnams kyis gzhi des glengs kyang dge slong rnams kyi bsgo ba rnal gzon na/gal te dge 'dun gyis dus la bab cing bzod na 
4b2('dul ba): dge 'dun gyis gnang bar mdzod cig dang /dge 'dun gyis tshe dang ldan pa 'dun pa bsgo ba rnal gzon pa'i grangs su stsal to//'di ni gsol ba'o//gang gi tshe na tshe dang ldan pa 'dun pa sgo ba rnal gzon pa'i grang su bcug pa de'i tshe na des 
4b3('dul ba): bsams pa/bdag la nyes pa gang byung ba de ni bdag gis de dag la tshig smras pa las gyur pa yin te/gang gis/skyes bu mkhas dang ldan pa yis//dag rnams bzhi po gsung bya ste//nags kyi nang du gong ma sreg/sgra 'byin pas 
4b4('dul ba): ni zin la ltos//shes smras pa ni legs par smras pa yin no snyam nas/de dge slong rnams kyis smras pa na cang mi smra bas/dge slong rnams kyi bsgo ba la gzon par byed nas/dge slong don nyung ngu dag ci'i phyir dge slong dag dge slong 
4b5('dul ba): rnams kyis smras pa na cang ma smra bas dge slong rnams kyi bsgo ba/la gzon par byed ces smod par byed/phyar ka gtong bar byed/kha zer bar byed pa'i skabs de dge slong rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das 
4b6('dul ba): kyis bka' stsal pa/dge slong dag khyed kyis dge slong 'dun pa'am gzhan yang de lta bu gang yin pa de cang mi smra ba bsgo ba rnal gzon pa'i grangs su chug shig/'di ltar gzhug par bya ste/gnas mal bsham pa byas nas gaN 
4b7('dul ba): DI brdungs te dris pa'i tshig gis dge slong rnams la yang dag par bsgo nas dge 'dun thams cad 'dug cig/'thun par gyur pa la dge slong gcig gis gsol ba bya ste/dge 'dun btsun pa rnams gsan du gsol/tshe dang 
4b8('dul ba): ldan pa 'dun pa 'di ltung ba byung yang chos bzhin du slar mi bgyid de/'di la dge slong don 'tshal ba/phan pa 'tshal ba/bde ba 'tshal ba/bde ba la reg par 'tshal ba/grub pa dang bde ba 'tshal ba rnams kyis gzhi des glengs

5b1('dul ba): tshig dang tha snyad dang /skad dang /rjod pa dang /nges pa'i tshig dang /tshig gi lam dang /tshig gi sgra dang /ngag gi las dang /ngag gi rnam par rig byed rjod pa'o//ltung byed do zhes bya ba ni sems can dmyal ba dang /dud 'gro dang /yi dgas dang /ngan song dang /

5b3('dul ba): so//de la ltung bar ji ltar 'gyur zhe na/dge slong dge slong gi gan du 'ongs te 'di skad ces tshe dang ldan pa khyod kyis dge slong skra dang kha spu bregs nas ring po ma lon pa snam sbyar dmar ser dang lhung bzed nya nye'i dbyar mdab kyis sbyar ba dang /khar 
5b4('dul ba): ba sbu bus dkris pa dang /zhun mar gyi lhung gzed dam/'bru mar gyi lhung bzed dam/sbrang rtsi'i lhung bzed dam/bu ram gyi dbu ba'i lhung bzed kyis 'ong ba mthong ngam zhes zer ba la/des 'di skad ces tshe dang ldanapa kho bos dge slong skra dang kha 
5b5('dul ba): sbu bregs nas ring po ma len pa snam sbyar dmar ser gnyis dang lhung bzed nya nye'i dbyer mdab kyis sbyar ba dang khar ba sbu bus dkris pa dang/zhun mar gyi lhung bzed dam/'bru mar gyi lhung bzed ngam/'bru mar gyi lhung bzod dam/sbrang rtsa'i lhung bzed dam/bu 
5b6('dul ba): ram gyi dbu ba'i lhung bze dam/'di ltar kho bos dge slong rkang pa gnyis kyis 'ong ba mthong ngo zhes smras na ltung byed du 'gyur ro//dge slong dge slong gi gan du 'ongs te/'di skad ces tshe dang ldan pa khyed kyis

5b8('dul ba): 'ongs pa mthong ngam zhes zer ba la/des 'di skad ces tshe dang ldanapa kho bos dge slong ma skra bregs nas/ring po ma lon pa snam sbyang dmar ser dang lhung bzod zha nye'i dbyar mdab kyis sbyar ba dag zhun mar gyi lhung bzed dam/'bru 
6a1('dul ba): @#//mar gyi lhung bzed dam/sbrang rtsi'i lhung bzed dam/bu ram gyi dbu ba'i lhung bzed kyis 'ong ba ni mthong gi/'di ltar kho bos dge slong ma rkang pa gnyis kyis 'od ba mthong ngo zhes smras na ltung byed du 'gyur ro//dge slob ma dang dge tshul 
6a2('dul ba): dang /dge tshul ma la yang de bzhin du ci rigs par sbyar ro//dge slong dge slong gi gan du 'ongs te/'di skad ces tshe dang ldan pa ji ltar khyim pa bu ram shing gi khur gyis sam/smig mi'i khur gyis sam/'dam bu'i khur gyis sam/shing gi khur 
6a3('dul ba): gyis sam/zhun mar gyi bum pas sam/'bru mar gyi bum pas sam/sbrang rtsi'i bum pas sam/bu ram gyi dbu ba'i bum pas 'ong ba mthong dam zhes zer ba la/des 'di skad ces tshe dang ldan ba kho bos khyim pa bu ram shing gis khur gyis 
6a4('dul ba): sam/smig ma'i khur gyis sam/'dam ba'i khur gyis sam/shing ga khur gyis sam/zhun mar gyi bu ma pas sam/'bru mar gya bum pas sam/sbrang rtsi' bum pas sam/bu ram gya dbu ba'i bum pas 'ongs pa ni ma mthong gi/'di ltar 
6a5('dul ba): kho bos khyim pa rkang pa gnyis kyi 'ong ba mthong ngo zhes smras na ltung byed du 'gyur ro//dge slong dge slongag gi gan du 'ongs te//'di skad ces tshe dang ldanapa ji ltar khyim pa mo gos thams cad sngon pos sam/thams cad ser pos sam/thams cad 
6a6('dul ba): dmar pos sam/thams cad dkar pos sam/zhun mar gyis bum pas sam/'bru mang gyi bum pas sam/sbrang rtsa'i bum pas sam/bu ram kyi dbu ba'i bum pas 'od ba mthong dam zhes zer pa la des 'di skad ces tshe dang lngan pa kho bos 
6a7('dul ba): khyim pa mo gos thams cadasngon pos sam/thams cadaser pos sam/thams cadadamar pos sam/thams canadadakar pos sam/zhun maragyi bum pas sam/'bru maragyi bum pas sam/sbrang rtsi'i bum pas sam/bu ramagyi dbu ba'i 
6a8('dul ba): bum pas 'ong ba ni ma mthong gi/'di lta kho bos khyim pa mo rkang pa gnyis kyi 'od ba mthong ngo zhes smras naltung byed du 'gyur ro/dge slong gis ji tsam du gzhan 'dri ba la lan gzhan 'debs na thams cad du ltung byed du 'gyur ro//rnam 
6b1('dul ba): grangs de dag thams cad la cad mi smra pas rna la gzon pa na nyes byas su 'gyur ro//gshed ma'i gsad par bya ba bros nas dge slong gi gan du 'ongs te 'phags pa ji ltar gsad par bya ba mthong ngam zhes 'dri na/gal te grang ba'i dus yin na dge 
6b2('dul ba): slong gis gshed ma la bzhin bzangs me khang du zhugs la me 'di shig ces brjod par bya'o//gal te tsha ba'i dus yin na bzhin bzangs je dpong sa'i khang par zhugs shig/je skom 'thung zhig/je dal so shig ces brjod par bya'o//gal te des de 
6b3('dul ba): skad ces bdag mi grang ngo //bdag mi tsha'o//bdag mi skom mo//bdag ma chad do//'di ltar bdag gsad par bya ba 'di'o zhes zer na/je sen mo la bltas te sen mo mthong ngo zhes brjod par bya'o//nam mkha' la bltas te nam mkha' mthong ngo 
6b4('dul ba): zhes brjod par bya'o//gal de 'di skad ces bdag sem mo mi 'dri'o//bdag nam mkha' mi 'dri'o//'di ltar bdag gsad bar bya ba 'dri'o zhes zer na/dge slong des phyogs bzhir rnam par bltas te 'du byed thams cad bdag med pa yid la byas 
6b5('dul ba): nas don dam par na sems can ma mthong ngo zhes brjod par ba la ltung ba med do//kha na ba dang /khas sman 'dzin pa la ltung ba med do//las dang po pa dang /smyos pa dang /sems 'khrugs pa dang /tshor ba dag gis nyen pa la ltung ba med do 
6b6('dul ba): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das la dge slong rnams kyis zhus pa/btsun pa tshe dang ldan pa 'dun pa tshangs pa mtshungs par spyod pa rnams kyi ngag mi nyan nas bsgo ba rnal gzon pa dang /cang mi smra bas rnal gzon pa'i grangs su stsal pa la gzigs dge slong dag da ltar du ma zad 
6b7('dul ba): kyi 'di ltar 'das pa'i dus na yang 'di dge ba'i bshes gnyen rnams kyi da dag mi nyan nas tshul ma yin pas sdug bsngal bar 'gyur te/nga nyon cig/dge slong dag sngon byung ba'i dus na rdzing zhig na ru sbal gcig dang dad pa gnyis 
6b8('dul ba): shig 'khod de/de nas dus gzhan zhig na lo bcu gnyis su than pa byung nas des rdzing de bskam par brtsams so//chos nyid kyis na 'jig rten gnas pa chags nas ring po ma lon pa'i tshe na dud 'gro dag kyang smra shes 
7a1('dul ba): @#//pas dad pa gnyis kyis ru sbal la smras pa/grogs po kho bo cag gnyis ni rdzing gzhan du 'gre'o//ru sbal gyis smras pa/grogs po gnyis kho bo yang khrid de song shig/dad pa gnyis kyis smras pa/khyod ji ltar khrid de 'gro bar bya/
7a2('dul ba): ru sbal gyis smras pa/kho bo shing gi dbus su khas gzung bar byas/khyed gnyis kyis rtse mo gnyis nas zung la deng shig/dad pa gnyis kyi smras pa/khyod smra 'dod pa yin pas tshig srung mi nus kyis kho bo cag gnyis khyer te mi 'gro'o//ru 
7a3('dul ba): sbal gyis smras pa/tshig bsrung bar bya'o//dad pa gnyis kyis ru sbal de khyer te steng gi nam mkha' la 'phags nas ji tsam na ri brags shig gi steng na phar song ba dang /khye'u dang bu mo dag gis mthong nas de dag gis smras pa/shes ldan dag ltos shig/
7a4('dul ba): dad pa gnyis kyis ru sbal zhig khyer te song ngo zhes zer ba na/ru sbal des kyang dang du blangs so//de nas ri brags gzhan zhig gi steng na phar song ba ba dang /yang khye'u dang bu mo dag gis ma thong nas de dag gis smras ba/shes ldan dag ltos shing /
7a5('dul ba): dad pa gnyis kyis ru spal zhig khyer te song ngo zhe nas zer ba dang /ru sbal des bsams pa/bdag gis yun ji srid du tho 'tsham pa mnags par bya snyam nas smras pa/dad pa gnyis kyis kho bo khyer ba ma yin gyi 'di ltar kho rang 'gro'o zhes byas pa dang
